- **research-lifecycle**: 当用户提出研究主题、想开始一项新研究、问"接下来该做什么"、或要求继续上次的研究流程时使用。本技能是科研工作台的元路由器：把一个研究主题拆解为 question（问题界定）→ literature（文献调研）→ hypothesis（假设形成）→ experiment（实验/计算）→ analysis（数据分析）→ writing（写作成稿）六个阶段，逐阶段调用相应插件的 skill 执行，每个阶段结束经 stage-gate 门禁审批后进入下一阶段。同义触发场景：开始研究、开题、走流程、研究流程、lifecycle、继续上次的研究、阶段推进、研究项目管理、六阶段、从选题到成稿。 ([MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/Hylouis233/openscience/skills/research-lifecycle) · ★25 · 3 copies)

- **research-matt-p**: 高信頼の一次情報源に基づいて質問を調査し、結果をリポジトリのMarkdownファイルとして記録します。ユーザーがトピックの調査、ドキュメントやAPIの事実の収集、または読み取り作業をバックグラウンドエージェントに委任したい場合に使用します。 ([seiei-sogen/agent-skills](https://github.com/seiei-sogen/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/matt/engineering/research-matt-p))

([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/research-mattjefferson-agents) · ★745)
- **research-mcp**: Local MCP aggregator (Zotero + ZotSeek + PaperKit + pp-scite) exposed to ChatGPT and other MCP clients through a private OpenAI tunnel or Cloudflare OAuth. Use when connecting a remote MCP client to Scite, private Zotero, or ZotSeek search, or when the user says my-research-library / research-mcp / ChatGPT research connector. ([dahliasan/researchskills](https://github.com/dahliasan/researchskills/tree/HEAD/skills/research-mcp) · ★2)
- **research-md-compactor**: Convert long verbose research Markdown into compact reference-grade knowledge artifacts preserving substantive content at field level. Use when user asks to compact, compress, normalize, densify, deduplicate, or make deep-research LLM-context friendly. ([Biskiq/spatial-sketch-editor](https://github.com/Biskiq/spatial-sketch-editor/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/research-md-compactor) · ★1)
- **research-md-maker**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "research a topic", "create a research doc", "explore options for X", "what are the different approaches to X", "write up research on X", or needs a broad landscape scan on any subject. Produces a concise, well-segmented .md research file covering a diverse range of options, approaches, or findings. ([fangdav/pocket-space-plugins](https://github.com/fangdav/pocket-space-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/pocket-space/skills/research-md-maker))
- **research-me**: Use when the user wants an interactive research partner that asks focused questions while independently searching current papers, articles, and open-source repositories. ([JunkaiWang-TheoPhy/research-me-skill](https://github.com/JunkaiWang-TheoPhy/research-me-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **research-measurement**: Source-grounded reference for the Math 4 Wisdom page /wiki/Research/Measurement titled "Measurement". Listed under "Physics" in the index at https://www.math4wisdom.com/wiki/Research/Index. Use when a task needs what this page states, cited to its URL, kept separate from the agent's own interpretation. ([docxology/Skillarum](https://github.com/docxology/Skillarum/tree/HEAD/output/skills/Math4Wisdom/Research-Measurement) · ★1)
- **research-mechanism-analysis**: 从研究对象的原理推导约束、部署选择、改动效果和可复算公式，并检查理论与实测的边界。用于原理讲不清、公式不可信、结论缺因果支撑的报告。 ([cyl19970726/creator-lab](https://github.com/cyl19970726/creator-lab/tree/HEAD/workbenches/research/.agents/skills/research-mechanism-analysis))
- **research-mechanism-diagnosis**: 对已界定的研究问题、失败实例或反常现象进行机制诊断，建立竞争解释、区分性预测和可干预位置。适用于定位根因、分析为什么失败、确定先查什么；不把合理故事当因果结论，不自动设计完整方法或启动实验。 ([EfficientAI-USTC/research-skills](https://github.com/EfficientAI-USTC/research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research-mechanism-diagnosis) · ★1)
- **research-mechanism-innovation**: 算法与系统科研中的机制创新方法。把初始 Idea、已有实验和失败结果转化为值得研究的问题； 通过估计改进空间、拆开现象和有证据的观察构造新的机制、模型或结构，并用鉴别实验检验。 用户要求寻找创新点、设计新机制或新方法、突破已有权衡、解释无收益的实验或推进研究方向时使用。 执行研究实验后、汇报结果前也应使用；结果无收益、准备得出某方向无效的结论时必须使用。 ([Sombrer0-1/MySkills](https://github.com/Sombrer0-1/MySkills/tree/HEAD/deep-research))
- **research-mechanism-necessity-check**: name: research-mechanism-necessity-check ([zms618/no-heartburn-academic-skills](https://github.com/zms618/no-heartburn-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research-idea-discovery/skills/research-mechanism-necessity-check))

Load when planning a study, doing a lit review, or assessing whether a claim is established. - **research-methodology**: Use when you need to know whether something is actually true and how sure you can be, not just what the internet says. For checking a claim, a statistic, a news story, a product promise, or a piece of research (including research your AI just did for you). The AI breaks the claim into parts, checks each against independent sources, looks for its own blind spots, and labels how confident it really is. Say "is this true?", "how sure are we?", "check this claim", or "research this properly". ([learnmagic-ai/skills](https://github.com/learnmagic-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research-methodology))
